    parent.scrollTop = itemTopRelative - parentRect.height / 2 + itemRect.height / 2;

dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">ADOS Drone Agent</span></h1>
<span data-as="p">The ADOS Drone Agent is the software that runs on the companion computer inside your drone. It sits between the flight controller (ArduPilot, PX4, Betaflight, or iNav) and everything else: your ground station, your phone, and the cloud.</span>
<span data-as="p">It is a hybrid system. The core long-running services (the supervisor, MAVLink router, HTTP front, cloud relay, logging daemon, and video pipeline) are native Rust binaries. Python handles AI and vision inference, hardware detection and first-boot bootstrap, the plugin runtime, and the setup and update webapp, all served behind the Rust front. It is licensed under GPLv3 and runs on ARM single-board computers from Raspberry Pi to Rockchip to NVIDIA Jetson.</span>

aria-hidden="true" class="size-4 shrink-0"><path d="M10.75 2.75H15.25V7.25" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M15.25 2.75L10.75 7.25" [&amp;_a]:border-b [&amp;_a]:border-primary [&amp;_a:hover]:border-b-2 dark:[&amp;_a]:text-white dark:[&amp;_a]:border-primary-light" data-component-part="frame-caption"><p>The local setup webapp showing agent setup and access URLs</p></figcaption></figure></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="what-it-does"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#what-it-does" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">What it does</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">The agent handles four core jobs:</span>
<ol>
<li>
<span data-as="p"><strong>MAVLink proxy</strong> connects to your flight controller over serial UART, then forwards telemetry to WebSocket and TCP clients. Your GCS and the cloud all get MAVLink data without fighting for the serial port.</span>
</li>
<li>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Video pipeline</strong> detects your camera (CSI or USB), encodes H.264 via hardware or software, pushes it to a local RTSP server (MediaMTX), and optionally transmits over WFB-ng for long-range HD video.</span>
</li>
<li>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Cloud relay</strong> sends telemetry and receives commands through three layers (Convex HTTP polling, MQTT at 2 Hz, and WebRTC peer-to-peer signaling) so your drone is reachable from anywhere.</span>
</li>
<li>
<span data-as="p"><strong>Service supervisor</strong> manages all of the above as individual systemd services with automatic restart, resource limits, and health monitoring.</span>
</li>
</ol>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="key-numbers"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#key-numbers" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Key numbers</span></h2>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_24llktbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Metric</th><th>Value</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Current version</td><td data-numeric="true">0.89+</td></tr><tr><td>Language</td><td>Rust + Python 3.11+</td></tr><tr><td>License</td><td>GPLv3</td></tr><tr><td>Supported boards</td><td>17 board profiles</td></tr><tr><td>Systemd services</td><td>Air + ground (the active set varies by profile)</td></tr><tr><td>REST API port</td><td>8080 (native Rust front)</td></tr><tr><td>IPC sockets</td><td><code>/run/ados/mavlink.sock</code>, <code>/run/ados/state.sock</code></td></tr><tr><td>Config file</td><td><code>/etc/ados/config.yaml</code></td></tr><tr><td>Install method</td><td>Mission Control <a class="link" href="/mission-control/flash-tool">Flash Tool</a> install command (<code>curl | bash</code>)</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="architecture-at-a-glance"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#architecture-at-a-glance" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Architecture at a glance</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">The agent uses a multi-process architecture managed by systemd. Each service runs in its own process with its own cgroup resource limits. The supervisor service (<code>ados-supervisor</code>) is a Rust orchestrator that starts and monitors child services through <code>systemctl</code>. Most units now run a native Rust binary; a few stay Python by design (see the table below).</span>

style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="min-w-full! h-full children:my-0! children:shadow-none! children:bg-transparent!"><div class="font-mono whitespace-pre leading-6"><pre class="shiki shiki-themes github-light github-dark" style="background-color:#fff;--shiki-dark-bg:#24292e;color:#24292e;--shiki-dark:#e1e4e8" language="text"><code language="text" numberOfLines="13"><span class="line"><span>ados-supervisor             # Rust: orchestrator (drives systemd)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span>  ├── ados-mavlink          # Rust: FC serial &lt;-&gt; MAVLink router (WS :8765)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span>  ├── ados-control          # Rust: HTTP front on :8080 (proxies the residual API)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span>  ├── ados-api              # Python: residual FastAPI on an internal socket</span></span>
<span class="line"><span>  ├── ados-cloud            # Rust: MQTT + Convex cloud relay</span></span>
<span class="line"><span>  ├── ados-logd             # Rust: black-box logging (WAL SQLite)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span>  ├── ados-health           # Python: CPU, RAM, temp, disk monitoring</span></span>
<span class="line"><span>  ├── ados-video            # Rust: camera + encoder + MediaMTX</span></span>
<span class="line"><span>  ├── ados-wfb              # Rust: WFB-ng video transmitter</span></span>
<span class="line"><span>  ├── ados-vision           # Rust: vision host</span></span>
<span class="line"><span>  ├── ados-plugin-host      # Rust: plugin subprocess supervisor</span></span>
<span class="line"><span>  └── ados-discovery        # mDNS advertisement</span></span>
<span class="line"><span></span></span></code></pre></div></div></div></div><div class="code-block-fade-overlay print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#fff;--fade-color-dark:#24292e"></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Services communicate through two Unix domain sockets:</span>
<ul>
<li><code>/run/ados/mavlink.sock</code> carries raw binary MAVLink frames (4-byte length prefix + payload)</li>
<li><code>/run/ados/state.sock</code> carries JSON telemetry at 10 Hz</li>
</ul>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="hardware-auto-detection"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#hardware-auto-detection" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Hardware auto-detection</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">On first boot, the agent reads <code>/proc/device-tree/model</code> and matches it against 17 board profiles stored as YAML files. Each profile defines the SoC, available buses (UART, I2C, SPI, USB), video encoding capabilities, GPIO pins, and a default tier.</span>
<span data-as="p">The tier determines which features are enabled:</span>
<!-- --><div role="presentation" style="position:relative;--scroll-area-corner-height:0px;--scroll-area-corner-width:0px" data-component-part="scroll-area" data-table-wrapper="true" class="min-w-0 [--page-padding:20px] flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-(--page-padding) max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div role="region" data-id="base-ui-_R_4cllktbsnlhjiuqsnpfiutb_-viewport" tabindex="-1" class="size-full rounded-[inherit] [--scroll-area-fade-size:32px] overflow-y-hidden! base-ui-disable-scrollbar" style="overflow:scroll" data-component-part="scroll-area-viewport" aria-label="Scrollable table"><div role="presentation" style="min-width:fit-content" data-component-part="scroll-area-content" class="flex"><div class="px-(--page-padding) grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px]"><thead><tr><th>Tier</th><th>RAM</th><th>Features</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td data-numeric="true">1</td><td>64-128 MB</td><td>MAVLink proxy only</td></tr><tr><td data-numeric="true">2</td><td data-numeric="true">256-512 MB</td><td>+ REST API, basic telemetry</td></tr><tr><td data-numeric="true">3</td><td>1-2 GB</td><td>+ video pipeline, cloud relay</td></tr><tr><td data-numeric="true">4</td><td>4 GB+</td><td>Full autonomy, vision engine, plugin sandbox</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div></div></div>

[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-green-800 dark:text-green-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">You do not need to configure the tier manually. The agent detects your board and picks the right tier automatically. You can override it in <code>/etc/ados/config.yaml</code> if needed.</span></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="two-profiles-one-codebase"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#two-profiles-one-codebase" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Two profiles, one codebase</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">The same agent binary supports two profiles:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>Drone profile</strong> (default): runs on the companion computer mounted on the drone. Manages FC, camera, video TX, and cloud telemetry.</li>
<li><strong>Ground station profile</strong>: runs on a ground-side SBC (typically a Raspberry Pi 4B). Receives WFB-ng video, creates a WiFi hotspot, serves video to your laptop or phone via WebRTC, and optionally drives an HDMI display and OLED screen.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">The profile is detected automatically at boot based on hardware fingerprinting. If the SBC has an OLED on I2C, four GPIO buttons, an RTL8812EU WiFi adapter, and no flight controller on UART, it picks the ground station profile.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="interfaces"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#interfaces" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="size-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-xs text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 18 18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true" class="size-3 shrink-0"><path d="M8.50001 6.827C8.14801 6.995 7.81801 7.225 7.52701 7.517L7.51701 7.527C6.13601 8.908 6.13601 11.146 7.51701 12.527L9.69201 14.702C11.073 16.083 13.311 16.083 14.692 14.702L14.702 14.692C16.083 13.311 16.083 11.073 14.702 9.692L13.771 8.761" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path><path d="M9.50002 11.173C9.85202 11.005 10.182 10.775 10.473 10.483L10.483 10.473C11.864 9.092 11.864 6.854 10.483 5.473L8.30802 3.298C6.92702 1.917 4.68902 1.917 3.30802 3.298L3.29802 3.308C1.91702 4.689 1.91702 6.927 3.29802 8.308L4.22902 9.239" stroke="currentColor" width="1.5" linecap="round" linejoin="round"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="min-w-0 break-words cursor-pointer">Interfaces</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">You can interact with the agent in four ways:</span>
<ol>
<li><strong>Setup webapp</strong> (port 8080): first-run identity, MAVLink, video, network, remote access, ground-station setup, logs, and advanced recovery.</li>
<li><strong>CLI</strong> (<code>ados</code> command): terminal status page, scriptable status, update, uninstall, plus subcommands for hardware, logs, network, plugins, profile, radio, and runtime mode. Works over SSH.</li>
<li><strong>REST API</strong> (port 8080): the native Rust front (<code>ados-control</code>) serves the REST API and reverse-proxies un-migrated routes to an internal FastAPI. Setup, telemetry, video, MAVLink, and command endpoints are available. Authenticated endpoints use <code>X-ADOS-Key</code>.</li>
<li><strong>ADOS Mission Control</strong> (GCS): the companion web app for maps, video, mission planning, hardware management, and fleet operations.</li>
</ol>

It sits between the flight controller (ArduPilot, PX4, Betaflight, or iNav) and everything else: your ground station, your phone, and the cloud.`}),`\n`,_jsx(_components.p,{children:`It is a hybrid system. The core long-running services (the supervisor, MAVLink router, HTTP front, cloud relay, logging daemon, and video pipeline) are native Rust binaries. Python handles AI and vision inference, hardware detection and first-boot bootstrap, the plugin runtime, and the setup and update webapp, all served behind the Rust front. It is licensed under GPLv3 and runs on ARM single-board computers from Raspberry Pi to Rockchip to NVIDIA Jetson.`}),`\n`,_jsx(Frame,{caption:`The local setup webapp showing agent setup and access URLs`,children:_jsx(OptimizedImage,{src:`https://mintlify.s3.us-west-1.amazonaws.com/altnautica/images/placeholder-setup-webapp.png`,alt:`ADOS setup webapp showing setup status and access URLs`})}),`\n`,_jsx(Heading,{level:`2`,id:`what-it-does`,children:`What it does`}),`\n`,_jsx(_components.p,{children:`The agent handles four core jobs:`}),`\n`,_jsxs(_components.ol,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.li,{children:[`\n`,_jsxs(_components.p,{children:[_jsx(_components.strong,{children:`MAVLink proxy`}),` connects to your flight controller over serial UART, then forwards telemetry to WebSocket and TCP clients. The supervisor service (`,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`ados-supervisor`}),`) is a Rust orchestrator that starts and monitors child services through `,_jsx(_components.code,{children:`systemctl`}),`.
